Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in galactic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 2 and the invariant I(A,B) = 733 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the galactic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 2 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 733. QED.
